\begin{center}
\vspace{5mm}
{\huge Monotonicity and extrema, group \textit{Alpha $\alpha$} -\romannumeral1}\\[5mm]
\begin{minipage}{0.95\linewidth}
\begin{center}
{\small \begin{multicols}{2} \raggedright
\textbf{(a)} Determine the monotonicity of the function \(f(x)\) in the neighborhood of the point \(x_0\). \\[1mm]
\textbf{(b)} Determine the intervals on which the function \(g(x)\) is decreasing. \\[1mm]
\textbf{(c)} Determine the number of stationary points of the function \(h(t)\). \\[1mm]
\textbf{(d)} Does the function \(v(x)\) attain a local extremum? \par \columnbreak
\textbf{(e)} Determine the difference between the values of the local maximum and the local minimum of the function \(\varphi(x)\). \\[1mm]
\textbf{(f)} Determine the product of the \(x\)-coordinates of the points of the global maximum and global minimum of the function \(\omega(x)\) on the given interval. \\[1mm]
\end{multicols}
}
\end{center}
\end{minipage}
\\[1mm]
\begin{minipage}{0.79\linewidth}
\begin{center}
\begin{varwidth}{\linewidth}
\setlength{\itemsep}{0pt}
\begin{enumerate}
\normalsize
\item $f(x)=\frac{x}{4}+\frac{4}{x} \enspace , \enspace x_0=-8$\quad \dotfill\; ???\;\dotfill \quad \text{increasing}
\item $g(x)=x^2\, e^{-4x+5}$\quad \dotfill\; ???\;\dotfill \quad $\left(-\infty , 0\right\rangle\enspace , \enspace \left\langle\frac{1}{2} , \infty\right)$
\item $h(t)=\cfrac{\ln t}{3t^4}$\quad \dotfill\; ???\;\dotfill \quad 2
\item $v(x)=7-\sqrt[7]{(3x+7)^2}$\quad \dotfill\; ???\;\dotfill \quad $\text{nie}$
\item $\varphi(x)=\cfrac{x^2-4x+29}{x-2}$\quad \dotfill\; ???\;\dotfill \quad $-20$
\item $\omega(x)=\frac{2}{3}x^3-\frac{9}{2}x^2+9x+6 \enspace , \enspace x\in\left\langle1 , 5\right\rangle$\quad \dotfill\; ???\;\dotfill \quad $12$
\end{enumerate}
\end{varwidth}
\end{center}
\end{minipage}
